- **llm-rag**: Build and optimize Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines. Use when someone asks the difference between an LLM, RAG, an agent and agentic AI, or when deciding how much machinery a task actually needs. ([dmavridi6478/business](https://github.com/dmavridi6478/business/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llm-rag-agent-agentic-ladder))

- **llm-readout-interference-bottleneck**: Mechanistic dissociation methodology from arXiv:2610.21176 (Yale, Gong 2026). Tests why LLMs fail N-back working-memory tasks: 15 pretrained LLMs (Pythia/GPT-2/Qwen2.5, 70M-14B) locate the target with attention but fail at readout — distractor letter content interferes with the decision variable, not attention precision. Sharpening attention barely helps (+0.05 d'); suppressing distractor letter-identity subspace raises d' in 42/43 cells (median +0.60). Includes formal propositions on when sharpening vs. interference-removal helps, and when softmax precision actually binds (single-layer trans… ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/nlp-llm/llm-readout-interference-bottleneck) · ★4)
